1b1(rgyud): @#//rgya gar skad du/de bI dzA li ma hA mA yA tan tra nA ma/bod skad du/lha mo sgyu 'phrul dra ba chen mo zhes bya 
1b2(rgyud): ba'i rgyud bcom ldan 'das gsang ba chen po la phyag 'tshal lo//'di skad bdag gis gsungs pa nyid du gyur pa'i tshe na/
1b3(rgyud): bcom ldan 'das de bzhin gshegs pa nam mkha' dang mnyan pa'i rnal 'byor chen po dang /rnal 'byor ma chen mo dang /
1b4(rgyud): shin du mos pa chen mo dang /mos pa dang bcas pa srung bar byed pa/rab tu rnam par srung bar byed pa nye ba mo dang 
1b5(rgyud): bcas pa nye bar zhi ba chen po dang zhi bar mdzad pa chen po mdzes pa dang mdog tu ldan pa/shes pa dang skyabs su ldan 
1b6(rgyud): pa ni 'di lta ste/gtso bo rnal 'byor 'khor lo zhes bya ba dang /rnal 'byor chen po rdo rje nyid du 'dus pa zhes bya ba dang /
1b7(rgyud): rnal 'byor chen po btang snyoms zhes bya ba dang /rnal 'phyor chen po dbang du mdzad pa zhes bya ba dang /rnal 'khor 
1b8(rgyud): shin tu rnam par rgya gram gyi tshul 'dzin pa dang bcas pa ni cir yang ston pa'i gzugs kyis kha dog bzhi dang ldan te 
2a1(rgyud): @#//'dug pa'i cha lugs kyis cod pan lnga dang ldan te//ma bsgribs mi sgrib sgribs pa mi 'gog bsgrib tu med pa'i dbyings nas yang dag pa'i gzhal yas chen po na mnyam pa chen po'i tshul rdo rjes sku ma bkag pas mdzad 
2a2(rgyud): pa bsam gyis mi khyab pas bzhugs so//de yang 'di ltar so sor gsal bar mnyam pa chen po dang 'khor dang bcas pa mnyam pa chen pos yo byad chen po ni kun gyi gdung 'dzin pa'i rang bzhin du gnas pa ni brten 
2a3(rgyud): pas phyin ci ma log pa dang gnyis du ma med pa ni/gzungs rnal 'byor ma chen mo ste/byang chub chen mo nam mkha' zhes bya ba dang /byang chub chen mo nam ma kha' 'dzin ma zhes bya ba dang /byang chung chen mo sa 
2a4(rgyud): srungs ma zhes bya ba dang /byangs chub chen mo brlan par byed pa chen mo dang /byang chub chen mo rnam par 'khengs pa spang pa zhes bya ba dang /byang cub chen mo las grub par mdzad ma zhes bya ba dang /byang
2a5(rgyud): tu chags pa chen po 'dzin zhes bya ba dang /rab tu ma chags pa gsod ces bya ba dang /byang chub pa chen mo rab tu rmongs sel zhes bya ba dang /byang chu ba chen mo gsung gi 'khor lo ma zhes bya ba ste//de dag kyang 'di lta ste/
2a6(rgyud): shin tu ud bal lta bu'i mdog dang /shag kra dang /bai du r+ya dang /pad ma rA ga dang /as ma gar pa la sogs pa rdzas kyi bsngags pas 'phags par 'tsher ba ma smin pa med par bzhugs pa ni rnal 'byor ma 
2a7(rgyud): kun du bzang po 'khor dang bcas pa'o//de yang gus pa chen po gsang ba dang /gus pa chen po sna tshogs pa'i dri dang /gus pa chen po dbang sgyur ba ni gsang ba'i dbang po zhes bya ba dang /gsal ba 'dzin byed ma 
2a8(rgyud): dang /rnam par dri'i gnas su gyur pa dang /rnam par sgra byed pa'i gzugs 'dzin pa'o//de nyid mthong bar byed pa'i dbang po dang /nyan par byed pa'i dbang po dang/bsung byed chen po'i dbang po dang /btar ba 
2b1(rgyud): chen po'i dbang po dang /shin du gtong bar mdzad pa dang /shin du rtog par mdzad pa dang /shin tu 'dzin dbang chen po dang /shin du grub par mdzad pa dang /cha lugs mi gtong ba'i btsun mo dang bcas pa'o|
2b2(rgyud): yod pa rnam par rtog par mdzad pa dang/med pa sel bar mdzad pa dang /rnam par 'du shes rgyal ba yin par mdzad pa dang /shing du 'gog pa rnam par 'joms pa chen mo dang /mngon spyod kyi las kyis gnyis 
2b3(rgyud): su med par 'khril nas chos kyi dbyings mnyam pa chen po'i gzhal yas na bzhugs so//de nas bcom ldan 'das gsang ba chen mo'i rnal 'byor ma'i gzhal yas su dgyes nas ma skyes pa'i dbyings shes 
2b4(rgyud): rab kyi tshogs chen mo mu khyud kun dang ldan pa/zla rgyas lta bu'i dkyil 'khor mtha' dbus med par khyab pa/rin po che kun dang ldan pa/grwa dang dos dang kha dog dang dbyibs rgya khyon dpag tu mang par gyur 
2b5(rgyud): pa|ye shes bsam gyis mi khyab pa'i cha lugs kyis rnam par mdzes pa/yid bzhin chen po'i rgyan gyis rnam par spras pa/sna tshogs pa'i bye brag gis rgyal ba rnams kyi gdung 'dzin pa/sgyu 'phrul 
2b6(rgyud): chen po'i sgo bzhi dang ldan pa/theg pa phyi'i rnal 'byor rnams kyi theg pa chen po 'bab gzhol dge sdig chen po'i mchog shin du gsang ba chen mo dang bcas pa/pad ma chen po'i dbus na lce kha dog bzhi'i bye 
2b7(rgyud): brag gis mdzas par gyur pa/phyogs bzhi breb rim pa gsum gyi cha lugs kyis gsang ba chen po'i dkyil 'khor du gyur pa'o//de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi dkyil 'khor sgrib pa mchog tu sel 
2b8(rgyud): ba ste le'u dang po'o////de nas bcom ldan 'das longs spyod chen po gdung ba mchog tu sel bar mdzad pa'i ting nge 'dzin la snyoms par zhugs te/'dzum pa chen pos rab tu chags pa chen po la 
3a1(rgyud): @#//
3b1(rgyud): kun 'dus mchog/pad ma rigs kyi phyag rgyar gsal//mun pa sel ba'i mar me bzhin//gsal dag yid gnyis med par bsgom//chu skyes 'dzum pa'i 'od du ldan//steg dang sprod pa'i rol mo yis//dgyes shing mnyam pa'i 
3b2(rgyud): mchod par bsham//'di ltar gsal bas dbang yang rdzogs//thams cad sdud pa zhes su bya//de bzhin dbang ste las thams cad kyi phyag rgya'i ye shes so//dus gsum pa yi mdzad pa che//rigs kun 'dus pa bde gshegs las 
3b3(rgyud): mnyam pa ji bzhin ting 'dzin te//yi ge 'bru gcig kun gyi mchog/bshad pas khyab par las kun rdzogs//chu skyes gsal ba'i 'dab ma ste//gnyis su med par gsal ba ni//rnam pa gnyis kyi bye brag gis//'dus pa 
3b4(rgyud): gsal bas skur gyur pa//'dod cing chags pa ma yin zhing //blo la yid gnyis med pa dang //phyag rgyar gsal bas rgyal ba'i sku//rgyal mo dang ni mnyam du dgyes//dbu gnyis pa yi yi ger ldan//sbyin pa rgya chen khengs pa na//
3b5(rgyud): rang gi 'dod pa'i gnas su bsdu//bong tshong lo grangs khyad par med//gnyis su med par 'khril bar gsal//dus kun du ni mnyam ldan pas//gsang ba'i mchod pa rab tu rdzogs//zla rgyas lta bu'i gtso bo bsgom//chags 
3b6(rgyud): pa med par 'dod sdud pa'i//yum dang lhan cig bsgom pa ni//A li kA li byed pa'i don//phyag rgya las kyi bye brag gis//bde ba chen po'i tshul gnas pa//rgyal ba kun kyang de bzhin 'thun//phyag lag snol mar gnas pa 
3b7(rgyud): yi//kun kyang sems dpa'i dkyil 'khor ro//phyag rgya thal mo rdo rje ni//kun kyang yum gyi rgyab tu snol//zhal gnyis mdzes par rab rgyas nas//zla gam pa yi ljags gnyis mdzes//dung gi phur bus 'khur chos la//gdabs 
3b8(rgyud): shing sngabs pa'i tshul du bzhugs//bsnyen pa nye bsnyen dbang rdzogs pa//rgyal ba kun gyi sku yis mchog/bzhad dang 'dzum pa'i gtso gzungs bsgom//rnal 'byor dka' thub chen po'i don//gsang ba sprod la shin du 
4a1(rgyud): @#//dgyes//gsang ba nyid du sems can 'dren//thams cad kun gyi rmongs pa sel//yum gyi las kyi khyad par ni//sa yi rim pa kun sbyong zhing //zas la bgo la dbang med pa//nyan thos 'gog pa la sogs 
4a2(rgyud): pa//byang chub sems kyi char chen gyis//dam por bcings pa tshar gcod mdzad//dam tshig chen po sdom pa ste//rgyal ba kun gyi dkyil 'khor yin//phyag rgya chen po'i tshogs mchog ni//dag pa don gyi rigs 
4a3(rgyud): mchog yin//myur bar rtag tu ma yengs na//gdon mi za bar 'grub pa ni//'dod khams lha yi nyin phyed kyis//gsang ba kun dang mnyam par 'gyur//de bzhin gshegs pa'i gsang ba mnyam pa zhes bya ba'i phyag 
4a4(rgyud): rgya'o//kun du bzang po rnal 'byor mchog/gsang ba nyid kyi mkha' dbyings nas//yi ge a ldan tsheg drag bcas//rnal 'byor bde chen thugs kyi sras//gzugs khams la sogs byin rlabs nas//phyag 
4a5(rgyud): mtshan 'khor lor gsal ba dang //bde ba rnal 'byor de bzhin gshegs//bcom gser gyi kha dog la//nyi ma zer gyi 'od bzhin du//gsal ba 'tsher ba bcom ldan 'das//dbu ru yi ge gnyis pa las//rin 
4a6(rgyud): chen myu gu khang ba 'bar//sangs rgyas lnga yi dbu rgyan gyis//legs par brgyan pa mtshan rnams rdzogs//rdzogs pa chen po'i bzhugs tshul dang //ting 'dzin 'thun pas 'gro la dmigs//pad ma ge sar dbus 
4a7(rgyud): gnas pa'i//thig bzhi gsang ba bzhi dang ldan//mdun du lha mo chags pa mo//lo grangs nyi shu mdzes dang ldan//ma hA bu ka'i gnas la dgyes//mdzes pa'i lag pa gar dang chas//gnyis su med pa'i bzhugs 
4a8(rgyud): chu la ni gtso la brtan pa'i tshul du bzhugs//rgyab tu lha mo dgyes chen mo//sangs rgyas brten par mdzad tshul gyis//thal mos 'dzin pa sku brtan pa//sku che bcu drug rtsa dmar sku//sangs rgyas gdung 
4b1(rgyud): 'dzin chags pas sbyong //'dod chags can la 'thun par mdzad//g.yas su zhe sdang gdul ba mo//rdo rje pad mor sbyor la dgyes//ma dad pa rnams dbang med par//drag por sbyor mdzad zhe sdang sel//chags pa 
4b2(rgyud): chen po bcom ldan 'das//gnyis su med par sbyor ba yis//gtong ba med par bstan pa'i tshul//sngo bsangs mdangs ldan khyad par che//khru tshad bcwa lnga chags pa can//dus mtha' med par sbyor la dgyes//
4b3(rgyud): steg dang sgeg pa'i cha lugs kyis//sems can kun gyi sdug bsngal 'khru//rab tu skams pa 'gog pa rnams//brlangs nas rgyal ba'i srasu 'gyur//ad twa ma li'i phrin las kyis//thams cad byang 
4b4(rgyud): chub chen por mdzad//mig gnyis 'dzums dang sbom po yis//dbang mor ldan par byas pa na//gnyis su med par sbyora'adod yin//sna yi rtse mor mtha'u chung 'dzin//ma btang bzhugs la brtson byos pa'o//
4b5(rgyud): so rnams bsdams nas kha phye ba//dus la bab ces bya ba ste//ting 'dzin gsal bar rgyu ba na//dus la bab ces bya bayin//'di ltar brda ldan ting 'dzin la//dam can bud med mi 'dud pa//dngos grub mi 
4b6(rgyud): 'gyur dam tshig nyams//tshe rabs kun du brlag par 'gyur//'di ltar brtsen par byed pa na//g.yan pa bslang ba rnam bzhi yis//skyes bu na chung tshul bzhin du//rdo rje pad ma'i 'tshams nyid tu dbyings//
4b7(rgyud): phyug lnga yi sbyor ldan pas//dal bus dal bus gzhugs pa dang //dal bus dal bus rab tu bskul//drag po'i yi ge spros pa na//byang chub sems kyi pad ma dgang //sbom po dbang ngam lnga rnams kyis//yum 
4b8(rgyud): gyis blangs te gtso la mchod//nye ba'i bsnyen pa la sogs pas//bsnyen pa che chung rdzogs par bsgom//byang chub sems kyi dkyil 'khor las//mchod pa brgyad kyis bstod par mchod//de ltar longs spyod 
5a1(rgyud): @#//rdzogs pa dang //gnyis su med par lung bzhin du//mnyam par 'khril ba'i tshul du bstan//dus gsum kun du gdung 'dzin pa//g.yon phyogs brten pa'i skal dang ldan//bong tshod lo ni bcu dgu pa'i//
5a2(rgyud): sdong po dul ba dag la dgyes//g.yas par bzur ba'i spyan gyis gzigs//dril bu sgra snyan khrol la brtson//rdo rje rab tu sgrogs byed ma//sangs rgyas lnga yi dbu rgyan can//mngon du sbyor la rab brtson pa//brda dang 
5a3(rgyud): bcas pa'i gar gyis ni//sangs rgyas kun la slong mdzad ma//byang chub sems kyi sprin chen gyis//mchod pa chen pos rab mchod mnyes//sprul pa bsam yas mtha' klas pa//dus gsum 'khor lo bskor bar 
5a4(rgyud): bskul//ngo sor chags pa'i mdangs kyis ni//mnyes dang bcas pa'i gar gyis mchod//gru chad bzhi yi rim pa las//thos byed gsal byed gsung ba che//dri dang ldan pa la sogs pas//btsun mo dag dang thabs 
5a5(rgyud): gcig tu//gnyis su med pa'i tshul du bzhugs//bar khyams bzhi la gnas pa ni//thos byed dbang dang nyan byed dbang //dri mchog dbang du lta byed dbang //dus ni bzhi rnams lha mo bzhi//btsun mo'i tshogs dang 
5a6(rgyud): ldan par gnas//zhe sdang 'joms byed la sogs pas//sgo rnams kun du gnas pa ni//snying rje'i yum dang bcas par gnas//nyi ma zla ba pad ma mchog/rin chen dung dang bcas pa ste//tshangs pa brgya byin 
5a7(rgyud): gnyis gnyis kyis//bteg pa'i khri la rab tu bzhugs//rdzogs pa chen por kun kyang gnas//sems dpa' chen po'i bzhugs tshul du//kun kyang rab tu gnas par bstan//zhal ni kun du sal le ba//
5a8(rgyud): thugs ni kun du lhan ne ba//sku ni kun la khyab par gsal//e ma mi skye mi 'gag mchog/e ma mi sel mi len mchog/e ma tshad ngam drag shul spyod//e ma gshegs pa'i stabs dang bcas//e ma 
5b1(rgyud): mtshan ma sel bar ston//e ma gsung rab rgya mtsho sgrogs//e ma byol song spro ba bskyed//e ma sprul pa bsam yas mchog/e ma mya ngan 'das par mdzad//e ma gsang ba des don mdzad//
5b2(rgyud): e ma gzungs dang mnyam par sbyor//e ma ci yang mi mdzad mchog/ngo mtshar sprin las 'di dag gis//sa yi rim pa 'byongs par 'gyur//zhes sangs rgyas pa nyid mngon par sangs rgyas pa la gleng 
5b3(rgyud): ste mnyes par gyur to//de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi sku dang //gsung dang thugs kyi gsang ba rnal 'byor bde ba chos kyi 'khor lo zhes bya ba'i le'u ste gnyis pa'o////
5b4(rgyud): de nas bcom ldan 'das bde ba chen po kun tu bzang mo rnal 'byor ma la 'jug par dgyes te rab tu 'dris pa chen po dgongs pa dang /kun tu bzang mo rnal 'byorams zhal 'dzum pa dang bcas pa//e ma sems 
5b5(rgyud): dpa' thig le che//bag chags kun gyi bsnyen por gsungs//'di las 'jug pa'i chos 'ga' med zhes brjod do//de nas gsang ba chen mo dang gnyis su med par 'khril bar gyur te/g.yon pa bskyed pa rnam pa mang 
5b6(rgyud): pos mnyes pa dang pad ma sbas pa la sogs pa 'byung ba spel bar mdzad nas rab tu dgyes te glu 'di blangs so//e ma bde ba mchog las skyes pa phyag na rdo rje can//chos kun dngos po med par gsal ba la//'byung 
5b7(rgyud): nas chen pos ya mtshan 'byung bar mkhyen pa e ma ngo mtshar che//rgyu nyid stong bas 'bras bu btsal med pa'i//rgyu dang 'bras bu nyid kyang rgyal bas yong mi rnyed//stong pa nyid la dga' ba'i chags pa'i cho 'phrul ba//
5b8(rgyud): de phyir skye 'gag med ces rgyal ba rnams kyis 'di la brjod//chos kun dbyer med ma rnyings pa nyid las//kun gyi dga' ba 'byung ba rgyal ba kun bas shin tu 'phags shes brjod pas gsang ba'i mkha' la kun tu 
6a1(rgyud): @#//bzang po rnal 'byor 'jug par mdzad de shin tu rngam pa'i glu 'di blangs so//sgyu ma kun las 'phags pas dbyer med rnam par 'phrul byed pa//kun tu bzang po rnal 'byor de ni bder gshegs yum 
6a2(rgyud): chen kun gyi gsos//sdug bsngal kun sbyong bder gshegs yum la 'jug pa thugs dam yin zhes rgyal bas gsungs//oM hU~M mi 'gog ji yang mdzad pa nyid ni mi mdzad mnyam pa yin zhes rgyal bas gsung //'di 
6a3(rgyud): ni glu ru blangs ba yis//mya ngan 'das kun 'gro ba'i mgon//stong pa chos kyi dkyil 'khor la//dka' thub chen po thob par 'gyur zhes//gnyis su med par glengs so//de nas snying po mchog gi yi ge ni//dang po'i 
6a4(rgyud): gnas su bsgom par bya//de snying yi ge bzhi ba ste//rdo rje chen po gsol ba mchog/yi ge a ldan thig le che//chos kun 'dus par rnam par bsgom//dang por me long lta bu ste//mnyes pa'i kha dog shin tu 'bar//
6a5(rgyud): phyag lag 'khyud pa sprod pa yis//thams cad de dang 'dra bar gsal//oM hU~M su ra ta su ke dz+nyA na da oM A+o~M hU~M//bder gshegs rgyal mo mkha' dbyings su//gsal dag las rnams 'grub par bsgom//chu skyes pad 
6a6(rgyud): ma 'dab ma stong //bshad pa'i sgras ni rnam par brgyan//de steng chos kyi sku ru gsal//zla rgyas lta bu'i mkha' dbyings nas//thams cad bsdu ba gsum gyis bsdus//oM su ra twa su khe d+harma d+hA tu swa b+hA bA oM A hU~M/
6a7(rgyud): gnyis su med pa'i yi ge mchog/mi skye ba ni a dang ldan//byin rlabs rgyal ba kun gyi gnas//mchod pa brgyad dang gsal bar ldan//so sor gsal ba'i mkha' dbyings la//hU~M gi zer gyis reg par mdzad//
6a8(rgyud): oM su ra twa su khen pra dz+nyA b+ha k+Sha na swa b+ha bA oM A hU~M//byang chub sems kyi cher chen ni//yi ge gnyis pa rtsa dmar sku//gnyis su med par ldan pa na//bdud kun rnam par btul ba dang //dbyings kyi bde la gsal ba 
6b1(rgyud): ni/sgo dbye ba yi tshul gyis ni pad ma dbye ba sna tshogs bya//spro ba dang ni bsdu ba yis//bya ba nan tan ye shes bskyed//'di ltar rgyal ba kun mdzad pa//sprul pa bsam gyis mi khyab las//mchod pa mang po lha 
6b2(rgyud): mor ldan//oM su ra twa su khe na duSh+Ta dz+nyA na badzra swa b+ha bA oM A hUM/rnal 'byor skal ldan chen po ni//skal ngan kun gyi gsos su gsungs//gnyis su med pa'i byang chub sems//mang sa bshang gci rdzas 
6b3(rgyud): rnams ni//dogs pa med pa'i dngos grub ste//rgyal ba nyid la rgyal bas mchod//de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi gsang ba'i snying po las bag chags gtan la 'bebs pa gnyis su med par gleng 
6b4(rgyud): pa'i le'u ste gsum pa'o////de nas bcom ldan 'das gsang ba'i snying po rdo rje rnal 'byor ma dang //gnyis su med pa'i phyag rgya'i mchog ni 'di lta ste//sgyu 'phrul dra ba'i phyag rgya las//mdzes 
6b5(rgyud): par gsal ba yab yum bcu//sor mo phan tshun spel legs pa//rdo rje thal mo byang chub rgya//gsang ba rnal 'byor thugs kyi mchog/dam tshig sdom pa 'da' par dka'//rnal 'byor ba la a lI gsal//
6b6(rgyud): kun tu bzang la kar byed//lnga char zur du ldan pa ni//gcig tu gyur pas gar gyi gzhi//oM hUM traM AHhUM/mA A kA hUM rA/thal mo btang snyoms tshul gyis ni//mdzes pa'i mtha' na gar gyis bya//snying kar bcings pas 
6b7(rgyud): rnal 'byor don//gar gyi phyag rgya dang nyid pas//gar du byas pas thal mo snol//mjug tu dbul ba'i tshul gyis bstod//rdo rje gar gyi phyag rgya las//g.yon pa rtse gsum g.yas pas gzung //de nyid gzhan 
6b8(rgyud): du 'bigs par bya//rdo rje gar gyi phyag rgya 'das//dga' ba'i mdangs dang ldan pa yis//g.yas su bdud pa'i tshul gyis ni//g.yas pa yi ni thugs kar bsgreng //g.yon pa gsung nyan 'khril ba'i tshul//rdo rje 
7a1(rgyud): @#//gar gyi phyag rgya las//mdzes pa'i mtha' nas gar byas shing //gzhan nas bsdus nas g.yas g.yon 'jog/rdo rje gar ni de dag las//lag pa g.yas pa 'degs pa dang //dbul ba'i tshul du rnam 
7a2(rgyud): par brtag/rdo rje gar ni de dag pas//spyan dang dbu ni g.yon du 'dud//cang te'u thogs pa'i tshul gyis ni//mchod pa byed par rnam par bsngags//rdo rje gar gyi phyag rgya las//lus ni g.yas g.yon bsgyur 
7a3(rgyud): ba dang /gar las lag pa bral bskyed pa'o//rdo rje gar gyi phyag rgyas las//g.yon pas g.yas pa'i gru mo gzung //g.yas pa nyug pa'i tshul du bya'o//de nyid rdo rje gar dag las//dam tshig phyag rgya 'thor bar 
7a4(rgyud): bstan//rdo rje gar gyi phyag rgyalas//dam tshig phyag rgya steng du bsgyur//de nyid g.yon pa'i nang dag las//g.yas pa'i dam tshig phyag rgyas gtor//de nyid g.yas pas drangs par ldan//de nyid gnyis pas gzung 
7a5(rgyud): ba yin//de nyid gnyis ka thal mo bsdam//de nyid dril bu 'dzin par bsgom//oM sarba ta thA ga ta/mu ka ka ra su khe na oM/oM sarba ta thA ga ta sa ma rA ga s+t+wa~M su ge na hU~M/oM sarba ta thA ga te dz+wa la yA nA na ma ma hA su ke na trAM/
7a6(rgyud): oM sarba ta thA ga te d+harma b+ha shra su khe na hA hA hA/de nyid gtso ba'i phyag rgyar bcas//oM sarba ta thA gA ta sarba bud d+ha lo tsa na Ad ma naM/sur twa su kha na maN+Da la pU dza ya pU dza ya oM oM oM/oM sarba ta tha ga ta 
7a7(rgyud): ma hA d+hu dzA dzA/oM sur twa su khe na b+han d+ha b+han d+ha/bU dza ya bU dza ya/hU~M hU~M hU~M A oM sur twa su khe na/man sha mo ha/pU dza ya bU dza ya/hri hri hri/oM sur twa su khen sarba ta thA ga ta d+harma dan tra/sA d+ha ka na da ho ho/oM

7b1(rgyud): bU dza ya bU dza ya/hrIM hrIM hrIM a/oM sarba ta thA ga ta shrI badzra nir te badzra gar+b+he ma hA ti la ka su ke na bU dza ya bU dza ya a a a/oM hU~M/oM sarba ta thA ga te shrI badzra du be/badzra gar+b+he su khe na bU dza ya bU dzra ya/oM tad ta da ta 
7b2(rgyud): hU~M/oM sarba ta tha ga ta shrI badzra puSh+pe badzra gar+b+he su khe na pU dza ya bU dza ya/hU~M hU~M hU~M aM/oM sarba ta thA ga to shrI badzra a lo ka dz+nyA na ma Na Ta la ti la ka bud d+ha su khe na bU dza ya pU dza ya/oM hriM hriM hriM oM A hU~M/oM sarba ta th+wa ga ta 
7b3(rgyud): shrI badzra gan+d+he badzra gar+b+he ma hA ma hA maN Da la ti la ka su ke na pU dza ya bU dze ya/a a a/oM a hU~M/de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad mnyes pa'i mchog las gar gyi le'u ste bzhi pa'o////
7b4(rgyud): de nas bcom ldan 'das dgyes pa chen mo dang bcas pa dam tshig bla na med pa'i rgyan bkod pa zhes bya ba'i ting nge 'dzin la snyoms par zhugs so//thams cad sdom pa la gnas par mkhyen no//dam tshig chen 
7b5(rgyud): po 'di dag gsungs//so so'i theg pa bsam yas pa//so sor nyes pa med na bde//'jig rten pa la chad mdo brtsan//'dul ba 'dzin la bsrungs na bde//gtsang sbrar spyod la gtsang sbrar spyad//mi dge zlog na dge 
7b6(rgyud): ba bcu//spyad pas lhag pa'i 'jig rten 'dzin//rang gi don gyi ting 'dzin gyis//byang chub sa la gnas pa'i rnams//nyams pa med de ma bcol na//nyams pa med de las kyang 'grub//rnal 'byor spyad pa 
7b7(rgyud): spyod pas 'grub//tshig cis goms pas theg pa 'joms//gtso mchog bshes gnyen mi spang zhing //bkur sti rim gro mchog kyang bskyed//ting 'dzin goms par cha lugs bsgom//tshig gi bzlas pa 
7b8(rgyud): bar mi gcad//dam tshig mched la gdung ba bskyed//sku yi dam tshig bsam mi khyab//gsung gi dam tshig bsngags pa yin//thugs kyi dam tshig lhag par gsungs//rigs lnga bsrungs ba'i dam tshig rnams//

8b1(rgyud): sdom pa med//rdo rje spun las dben par gdon//lha dang rdo rje slob dpon la//bag med bar ni brnyas pa dang //rmongs pas ma mthong g.yel ba dang //dam chos srog la mi lta ba//sangs rgyas rgyal ba bdun la brnyas//dkyil

8b3(rgyud): pa gang yang med//'di las 'das par mi byed pas//tshe 'di nyid la rgyal ba'i sku//lhun gyis grub pa'i sa la gnas zhes de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad nyid la nyid dam tshig chen po'i sdom pa 'dis 
8b4(rgyud): shin du bsnyengs par mdzad do//de bzhin gshegs pa thams chad kyi gsang ba'i bde ba sdom pa thams cad kyi mchog gi le'u zhes bya ste lnga pa'o////de nas bcom ldan 'das kun tu bzang po chen po 
8b5(rgyud): rnal 'byor ma dang bcas pa dgongs pa gcig tu gnas par mdzad do//rnal 'byor kyi dbang phyug chen po spyad pa mchog dang ldan pa'i spyod pa ma lus pa nyid dang zab mo'i don yin par mkhyen to//mkhyen nas sgyu

8b7(rgyud): bud med kun gyi sgyu ma las ni rab tu sprul te 'char//bden gnyis so sor snang ba nyid kyang yum gyi mkha' zhes rgyal bas gsungs//ma nor lam chen sgom na kun tu bzang mo bsgoms pas lam du zhugs pa yin 
8b8(rgyud): sangs rgyas kun gyi dgongs pa kun shes rab bud med las ni mchod cing bsnyen pas ye shes chen por 'grub bo//dkyil 'khor gdon pa'i las kyi dngos grub mchog gi la rdo rje glu'i don de nyid gnyis su med pa'i dgongs 
9a1(rgyud): pa la gsal bar gyur to//de nas de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi bdag nyid//dpal kun du bzang pos thabs dang shes rab kyi longs spyod 'di gsungs so//mngon spyod las kyis 'jug pa ni//phan 
9a2(rgyud): tshun du ni dga' spyod pa//rnal 'byor sdom la gnas pa yin//de la mchod pa dam pa ni//sbyor ba rab tu sbyor ba ste//kun du khyab pa kun du sbyor//rjes su chags pa dam tshig mchog/de phyir kar sham mdog dang ldan//
9a3(rgyud): bcu drug bod tshod ring ba la//lugs su blugs pa lta bu ste//lus legs mdzes dang ldan pa dang //dpral ba dbyes che kos ko phra//nu ma chung zhing sor mo ring //yid tsam bzur la mig dkyus ring //dkar zhing rtsa dmar 
9a4(rgyud): mdangs su chags//so dkar thags bzangs 'tshams thung zhing //skad sbom gdangs dang ldan pa ni//rdo rje sems dpa'i gnas mchog ste//'di la rtse bar byed pa ni//rigs kyi rdo rje gdon mi za//tshe 'di nyid la 'grub 
9a5(rgyud): par 'gyur//gnas rab dben par rab brtag ste//sha dkar mdangs ldan mdog dang ldan//lus 'bring zlum pa cha byad can//so dkar thags dam migs dkyus zlum//skad phra mdog dkar spro ring ba//dpral dbyes 
9a6(rgyud): la sogs bzang gnas pa//rma dag gnas pa la sogs ni//rtag par chos la 'dud pa yin//tshangs pa dbyangs kyi rigs can te//'di la brtson byas bsgrub pa yis//de bzhin rigs kyi ting 'dzin la//phan tshun 
9a7(rgyud): du ni dga' spyad na//tshangs pa'i snga dro gcig tsam gyis//gdon mi za bar 'grub par 'gyur//rnal 'byor sbyor ba dga' ba ni//sbyor ba brtson pas 'grub par gsungs//bud med cha lugs legs pa ni//bod tshod mi ring 
9a8(rgyud): pus pra zhing //rkang lag chung zhing skad gsal ba//mig legs ngo zlum rtsa dmar che//lhun du chags la mi shes med//lus la mtshan gyi rma 'dras brgyan//tshig gsal rmi lam nyung ba dang //sod kar mchu legs dad par 
9b1(rgyud): ldan//chos sems ba yi ting 'dzin gyis//gsang ba'i mkha' la nyi zla'i 'od//rdo rje pad mar ldan byas te//phan tshun du ni dga' spyad na//'dod khams lha yi skad cig gis//'grub par 'gyur ba gdon mi za//lus 
9b2(rgyud): zlum brtun dang nga rgyal bcas//khong na tshig rnon sems la 'dzin//bya bar dran la mi gtong ldan//bslab pa blo rtul sha rus che//mdangs bkrag brtags na phyi phyir che//sbyin la dga' zhing chos la dang //rin chen 
9b3(rgyud): rigs kyi ting 'dzin gyis//bag chags yi dges rtsad gcod cing //ting 'dzin blo la gsal ba na//bsgrub pa dus la bab pa ste//de tshe rab tu brtson pa yis//yum gyi pad ma kha phye ste//bde ba thugs kyi 'du 'phro yis//
9b4(rgyud): phan tshun du ni dga' spyad pa//rigs kyi rin chen 'grub pa ni//tshe 'di nyid la thob par 'gyur//thugs rje mdzad spyod bye brag gis//'jig rten dag na dman pa'i rigs//rab tu bkug ste dga' spyod na//snying 
9b5(rgyud): rje mchog dang thabs ldan pa//rnal 'byor spyod pa chen por gsungs//shan pa rigs ngan rmad 'tshong ba//ngan pa'i rigs dang longs spyod rnams//'joms pa po yis ting 'dzin gyis//myur du spyad na 'grub par 
9b6(rgyud): gsungs//yi dgas gnod sbyin byol song dang //kyi dang ru sbal spre'u la sogs//mi dang lha yi gnas mchog kun//so sor 'tsham bar dga' spyad na//dregs pa can la zhi ba che//brtson la dga' ba brtson par 
9b7(rgyud): mdzad//thabs kyi mdzad spyod kun gyi mchog/sems can 'di dag don gyi phyir//phyin ci log tu gang spyod rnams//'dul ba'i mdzad pa sna tshogs kyis//mi mdzad pa ni ci yang med//shin du gtum pa'i cha lugs kyis//gtum 
9b8(rgyud): po thams cad dbang du mdzad//shin du zhi ba'i cha lugs kyis//dregs pa can rnams dbang du mdzad//shin tu mdangs ldan cha lugs kyis//chags pa'i yid rnams dbang du mdzad/e ma shin du ngo mtshar che//
10a1(rgyud): ci yang yin pas cir mi dgongs zhes gsungs pas thams cad rab tu brtson pa chen po dang ldan par gyur to//bcom ldan 'das de bzhin gshegs pa bsam gyis mi khyab pa nam mkha' dang mnyam brdul

37b2(rgyud): gnas su gsor//mi dmigs bde dang spro ba 'di//kun du bzang mo dbyings su bskyil//zhes ched du gleng ste/gnyis su med pa las nyid kyi spyod yul thams cad gsang ba'i dkyil 'khor bla ma khyad par du sbags 
37b3(rgyud): pa rdul phra mo snyed 'thon nas sgyu ma'i cho 'phrul chen po bsam gyis mi khyab par mdzad pa ni/la la ni gsang ba bla ma'i dkyil 'khor du zhugs te yan lag thams cad rdo rjer byin gyis rlob/la la ni 'gro ba drug gdul

37b7(rgyud): ngan song sbyong //la la dkyil 'khor mchod sprin 'khril//kun kyang chos kyi 'khor lo skor//de ltar rtag tu chos kyi 'khor lo bskor ba ni 'di lta ste/theg pa dang po bcu drug mgon/gnyis pa bzhi yi lta spyod bsrung //gsum 
37b8(rgyud): pa bcu gnyis nges par bstan//bzhi pa gnyis kyi lta spyod sbyongs//bzhi pa'i theg pa mdor bsdus na//bla med theg pa'i dbyings su btsud//theg pa bzhi yi nges 'byung la//theg pa gcig gis 'bras bur gnas//
38a1(rgyud): @#//kun 'byung dkyil 'khor sgyu 'phrul che//mang la 'brel bas dra ba'i don//thun mong min pas gsang ba'i don//theg pa'i bcud de snying po'i dbyings//zhes ched du gleng ste/snod bcud kyi 'jig rten rgyal ba'i dkyil 'khor 
38a2(rgyud): gsang bar gnas la sgyur ba dang /phyogs bcu dus bzhi'i de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad gsang ba bla ma'i ting nge 'dzin gyi gzigs mos mnyes par gyur to//de nas snod bcud kyi 'jig rten thams cad dang /snyigs 
38a3(rgyud): ma med par gsang ba bla ma'i dkyil 'khor du mngon par sangs rgyas nas ched du brjod do//e ma'o rdo rje phung po'i yan lag ni//rdzogs pa'i sangs rgyas lnga ru grags//skye mched khams rnams mang po kun//byang chub sems

38a7(rgyud): cad mchog gi snying po bas//gzhan du rgyal bas chos mi btsal//nyid las gzhan zhes bya ba'i chos//btsal kyang rgyal bas mi rnyed do//zhes brjong pas chos thams cad ye nas sangs rgyas bar de bzhin gshegs 
38a8(rgyud): pa nyid kyis mkhyen to//de nas dbyings dang ye shes gnyis su med pa las ched du brjod pa 'di brjod do//e ma'o ye nas gsang ba'i chos//sna tshogs snang ba rang bzhin gsang //gzhan du min la shin tu gsang //
38b1(rgyud): gsang mchog snying po bla na med//thams cad gsang ba'i dbyings su nges//bla ma mchog gi snying po las//sangs rgyas sems can gnyis su med//ces brjod pas thams cad dbyings su nges par mkhyen to//de ltar yin 
38b2(rgyud): na yang 'gro ba'i rnam par rtog pas 'gro ba'i rigs bsam gyis mi khyab par smin pa la thugs rje chen po sangs rgyas kyi che ba'i ye shes bskyed pa'i phyir rdo rje bla ma'i tshig 'di ched du brjod do//e ma'o bde gshegs 
38b3(rgyud): snying po las//rang gi rnam rtog las kyis sprul//sna tshogs lus dang longs spyod dang //gnas dang sdug bsngal la sogs pa'i//bdag dang bdag gir so sor 'dzin//sus kyang ma bcings bcings med de//bcing bar 
38b4(rgyud): bya ba yod ma yin//rnam rtog bdag tu 'dzin pa yis//nan gyis mkha' la mdud pa por//bcings med rnam par grol med pa'i//ye nas lhun rdzogs sangs rgyas chos//bstan phyir spro ba sna tshogs mdzad//spros kyang 'phel 
38b5(rgyud): 'grib med pa'i dbyings//de ltar yin na yang de ltar ma rtogs pa rnams la kun rdzob kyi phung po byang chub kyi sems bla ma'i dkyil 'khor du sems kyi ngo bo bskyed pa'i phyir//rdo rje gsang ba'i tshig 'di ched du brjod de//stong 
38b6(rgyud): zhing dben pa'i sems nyid ni//rigs dang kha dog dbyibs med do//ci yang med la cir yang snang //mi bskyod rdzu 'phrul sna tshogs 'byung //rgyal ba'i dkyil 'khor sna tshogs kyang //sems kyi sgyu 'phrul chen po yin//gcig 
38b7(rgyud): dang du mar mi dmigs te//mngon shes drug gis thams cad byed//bla ma'i ting 'dzin chen po yis//dkyil 'khor chen po byed par bstan//rgyu lnga rkyen bzhis bsdus pa la//rgyal ba'i dkyil 'khor che phrag bsgom//
38b8(rgyud): bzhi yi rim pa tshul bzhin byed//rgyan dang phyag rgya'i tshul rnams kun//til 'bru'i nang du ri rab bskyed//sangs rgyas stong gi dkyil 'khor dang //stangs dbyal gnyis kyi phyag rgya yang //ri rab til 'bru'i tshul bzhin

40a1(rgyud): ba'i tshogs chen po//'bar ba'i phreng ba 'khrigs pa ste//mtha' dbus med par khyab pa yi//dkyil 'khor bsam yas lhun gyi grub//ho zhes brjod pas 'jig rten drug gi phyogs bcu mtha' yas

41b1(rgyud): dbyings//sngags dang phyag rgyar ldan pa yis//rdo rje gsung gi ngo bor shog/oM ma hA gu h+ya sarba ta thA ga ta tsi ta badzra swa b+hA ba Ad ma ke haM/hUM bde gshegs thugs kyi gtso bo las//mi shigs rdo rje rdo rje'i thugs//sngags dang 
41b2(rgyud): phyag rgyar ldan pa yis//rdorje thugs kyis byin rlobs shog/oM ma hA guh+ya sarba ta thA ga ta tsa ta badzra swa b+hA ba A da ma ke haM/swaM mi 'gyur ba'i zhing khams nas//ma bskyed pa ni 'bras bu lags//ma bsags pa yi longs

41b5(rgyud): dbyings//thams cad gsung gi phyag rgya mchog ho//gsang ba'i snying po des pa'i bla ma chen po las dkyil 'khor bsdu ba dang gsang sngags kyi le'u ste bzhi pa'o////de nas ye shes rgyal po de nyid de bzhin gshegs

44a1(rgyud): @#//'khor ma lus dang //rigs drug 'gro ba mngon du 'gyur//lus ngag sems dang chos thams cad//gang na'ang yod pa ma yin te//rnam dag ye shes sbyor ba yis//dbyings la dbyings nyid sgom pa yis//kun byas 
44a2(rgyud): dbang phyug rig pas 'jug/de nas dad ldan gus 'jug pa//de nas rigs kyi bu mchog des//rgyal srid dang ni rang gi lus//bu dang chud ma nor gyi dbyig/rab tu gces dang yid 'thad dbul//snying yang yon gyi mchog yin te//
44a3(rgyud): bla ma mnyes phyir gus ldan dbul//dbang phyug longs spyod lngas mchod na//dkyil 'khor thams cad mnyes pa yin//gsal ba'i dkyil 'khor smos ci dgos//skyon rnams ma lus nges par 'byung //dad ldan brtson 'grus

58a5(rgyud): le chen po'i lam du des//sgyu ma rol pa'i dkyil 'khor yang //thig le chen po'i dbyings su sbyor//sdom pa rnam pa bcu gsum dang //spyi yi dam tshig mtha' yas pa//thig le chen po'i ngang du gsal//snod bcud

58a8(rgyud): kyi ma dang dus kyi srid//thig le chen po'i ngang 'dus//snga phyi gsum dang yod med khams//sku gsung thugs su des bstan pa'i//thig le byang chub sems su des//phung po khams dang skye mched kyang //phyag rgya 
58b1(rgyud): bzhi bcu gnyis po yang /thig le nyid las gzhan na med//bzhi yi las dang gcig po yang //thig le sems kyi ngang du gsal//'gro ba'i don du 'byung ba yi//dgu dang stong phrag brgya bcu bzhi//byang chub sems las gzhan na

58b3(rgyud): gcig yab tu nges par rdzogs//thar pa'i lam mchog dbyings kyi yum//kun du bzang mo las sogs kyang //thig le ma yi ngang du gsal//gzhal yas khang dang dkyil 'khor dang //bzhugs gnas khyad par mang po yang //

58b5(rgyud): su nges pas na//dkar nag las kyis ma gsungs so//byang chub sems kyi bde chen ni//ci yang med las cir yang snang //cis kyang ma bcos g.yung drung sku//cir mi mdzad la thams cad sgrol//dmigs pa med par kun la

59b8(rgyud): byed pa yin//ljon shing dang ni lcug phran tshal//sdong gcig dang ni me reg tu//dang ba dga' ba chags pa dang //khros pas ka li rab tu bya//zhes gsungs pas phrin las rnam pa bzhi sti ra'i rnga dang bcas par phyogs bcu 'jig 
60a1(rgyud): rten gyi khams 'dul bar gyur te//de nas bcom ldan 'das ye shes rgyal po 'jigs byed chen po de nyid dam tshig sti ra'i rnga bla ma rdung zhing sti ra rngam pa'i mdangs 'di phyung ngo //e yi tshogs chen 'byam pa ru//sti

63a1(rgyud): @#//'bar//rtsa la me 'bar rked nas zhag kyang 'dzag/rtsa ba chu ste sdang mig rab tu bskrad//phyag drug phyag mtshan bsnams pa'i thor tshugs ni//bsdibs 'dzug rgyu zhabs lcags kyu g.yas//dung tshan mdung thung gsal

110b1(rgyud): spras//ka khag g+hang /tsa tsha dza dz+nya nya/Ta Tha D+ha Na/ta tha da d+ha na/pa pha ba b+ha ma/ya ra la wa/sha Sha sa ha k+Sha//ye shes ma 'dres bde chen gsal//rnam 'phrul rigs mchog gzungs kyi dam tshig dang //khro zhing 'dren pa'i 
110b2(rgyud): mdangs kyis gnas//rigs gnyis lung gis rgyud pa'i sku//dmar nag zhal gsum phyag drug pa//seng dkar dmar ba'i zhal gnyis dag/bzhi na mdangs tshangs pa'i tshul gyi gnas//dbus ma khro 'dzum brjid bag dang //

110b4(rgyud): dpe byad mdzes par brgyan//rigs kyi mchod pas dbu skra sil//rang 'od btsun mo'i sku bcas pa//phyung nas phyogs bcu'i sngags kyis spros//hU~M phaT pad mo 'grig dza swA hA//oM du~M du~M b+hi na d+ha dza pra mo haM/mdzad pa'i phrin las rdzogs 
110b5(rgyud): byas nas//slar 'dus bdag po'i sku la thim//shar du rnam snang phyag rgya che//gser mdog zhal gsum phyag drug pa//rtse gsum bai con pad mo ral gri ban d+ha'i dza na//lho ru rin chen 'byung ldan nyid//mthing ka zhal gsum phyag drug pa 
110b6(rgyud): rin chen pad ma gri gug dang //shu la bum pa rtse gsum 'dzin//nub tu snang ba mtha' yas sku//dmar po zhal gsum phyag drug pa//thod dbyug rtse gsum pad mo dang //dgra sta 'khor lo ral gri 'dzin//byang du don yod grub pa nyid//
110b7(rgyud): ljang gu zhal gsum phyag drug pa/ral gri rdo rje dbyug pa mdung //pad mo rtse gsum gshin rje'i rkang //kun gyi g.yas dkar g.yon pa ni//dmar ljang ser dang le'u rgan mdog/rang gi btsun mo'i 'gul nas 'khyud//zhal gcig 
110b8(rgyud): phyag gnyis bdag po ltar//sku mdog gsang ba'i mdangs dang ldan//phyag dang cha lugs kun gyis brgyan//oM dzi ni dzi ga la ma te//ra ti/swa ra da na d+hi ga/maM mo ha ra ti//oM a ro lig/byau rA ga ra ti/ha bra dz+nyA d+hi ga/
111a1(rgyud): tAM badzra ra ti/hU~M phaT swAhA/'od zer 'phro ba'i tshogs bcas pas//rigs kyi dkyil 'khor gsal bar mdzad//'khor lo sgyur rgyal cha lugs dang //dgyes pa'i dkyil dkrung tshul gyis bzhugs//khyams kyi phyogs dang 
111a2(rgyud): mtshams rnams su//sems dpa' brgyad dang sems ma brgyad//thugs kyi ye shes yi ge las//rang gi gsang sngags dag gis gdon//sa snying dkar ser rin chen 'dzin//nam snying mthing ka ral gri 'dzin//spyan ras dmar skya pad+ma 'dzin//
111a3(rgyud): gsang mdog ljang skya rdo rje 'dzin//g.yon pa pad mo yu ba can//bsnyems pa'i tshul gyis phyogs su 'dud//sgeg mo phreng ba dbyangs dang gar//dkar ser dmar ljang 'od 'phro mdog/me long rin chen phreng ba dang //dbyangs snyan gar 
111a4(rgyud): thabs mdzes pa'i tshul//oM k+shi ra dza ya/hU~M lA s+ye/sa ma ya s+t+wa~M/troM mA le sa ma ya ho/hri pad ma ban d+ha hri/gi rti ra ho ha~M/a ni ra ti ra ga la mi hU~M phaT/byams pa dkar ser klu shing 'dzin//sgrib sel 'khor lo mthing ka ste//'jam 
111a5(rgyud): dpal dmar skya ud pa la//kun bzang ljang ser rin chen snye//g.yon pa pad mo lcug ma 'dzin//sems dpa' stabs kyis gdan la bzhugs//bdug spos me tog 'thor ba dang //mar me dri chab lha mo rnams//ser dkar dmar ljang 
111a6(rgyud): go rim bzhin//mtshan dang 'thun pa'i rdzas kyis mdzes//oM ma ra ni/dza du ba pra be sha/thi ling swa ram pa ya/hU~M bus be a be sha hU~M swa ra tsa ya/ba di ba bu gi ni/muM shir tsi ya/ho gan d+he tsi ta ya hU~M phaT/shar sgor gshin rje 
111a7(rgyud): gshed po che//mthing nag be con rtse gsum phreng //lho sgor stobs chen ser skya'i mdangs//rdo rje zhags pa rin chen phreng //nub sgor rta mchog 'jigs byed rgyal//thod sbrul dril bu lu gu rgyud//byang sgor rdo rje 'khyil ba ste//
111a8(rgyud): ljang skya rdo rje rtse gsum mthu//khro bo cha lugs rngam brjid dang //dur khrod rim pa'i chas kyis brgyan//lcags kyu zhags pa lcags sgrog dril//spyan dmar 'jigs byed 'od dpung dang //rdo rje mche sogs khro gnyer can//yab 
111b1(rgyud): yum 'khyil ba brgyad bskum sku//hUM phaT ru lu'i gsung 'don cing //las kyi 'khor lo gnon pa'i tshul//oM ru lu pad+man ta kred hUM phaT/dza badzra kro ti shwa ri hUM badzra ta krid phaT//hUM rad na kro ti shwa ri/hUM pad man ta krid phaT/ba~M pad 
111b2(rgyud): ma kro ti shwa ri hU~M big nan ta krid//ha~M skar ma kro ti shwa ri hUM phaT swA hA//phyi nang med par dkyil 'khor chen po ste//thugs rje ye shes che las sprul//phyag rgya bzhi yi rgyas btab cing //dbu la rigs kyi bdag pos brgyan//'od dpung 
111b3(rgyud): sprul pa'i lha 'khor gyis//'jig rten gsum po snang byed cing //tshangs pa la sogs lha chen brgyad rnams 'gugs//rdo rje brjid gnon sngags kyi mthus//de dag dran pa med par 'gyur//mtshon cha'i tshogs kyis sgrol byas 
111b4(rgyud): te//dug gsum phung po gsol bar mdzad//phrin las khro bo brjid glu yis//lha chen 'khor rnams dbang du bsdud//dkyil 'khor chen por gzhag byas nas//snga ma'i sha rus gnan nas ston//rdo rje bdud rtsi'i ting 'dzin 
111b5(rgyud): gyis//lha chen rnams kyis slar lus gsol//'khor bcas pa yi dbang bskur zhing //rdo rje 'chang gi gral la dgod//dam tshig gsang ba'i brdas bsgros shing //phrin las bka' nyan gnyer gtad de//gzhal 
111b6(rgyud): yas khang gi rgyan du gzhag/kun kyang tshig dbyangs 'thun pa'i sgos//dam tshig mna' srog thal myong 'gyur//zhes brjod do//de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi dgongs pa bla na med pa'i gsang pa dpal rta mchog

112a4(rgyud): phyogs mtshams su//ye shes khro bo chen po brgyad//hri las grub pa'i 'jigs gzugs can//rta mgo ljang khu brjid pa yi//shar du pad ma'i dbang phyug ni//dkar po zhal gsum phyag drug pa'o//rdo rje pad ma klu zhags dang //

112a6(rgyud): gri rtse gsum lnga//rdo rje rtse gsum mda' dang gzhu//drag po gnas kyi sku dang 'khril//me ri 'bar bas kun du khyab//stag mo bcings pas gzhan gnas 'jig/sprul pa'i pho nya me 'brug gi/khro bo yin ni dkyil 'khor

112a8(rgyud): dri dang phong khrag 'dzin//byang du 'od byed 'jigs chen sku//ljang khu rtse gsum mnga' bo che//nor bu dung khrag lcug ma dung khrag 'chang //ra ro mig dmar zil gyis non//rgyal bdag khro bo'i sku dang 'khril//cod pan klu 
112b1(rgyud): mgo gdengs ka'i rgyan//dus mtshungs phud kyi sgra chen sgrogs//kun kyang thod phreng g.yang gzhi dang //dur khrod gnas 'byung cad me byed//'tshams kyi khro bo chen po bzhi//rigs kyi phyed gzugs thig les mtshan//zhal gsum phyag 
112b2(rgyud): drug 'od mdangs can//rdo rje thod dbyug glu yi sgra//lce spyang bya khyung gnam lcags sprin//thod phreng thig le dbyu gu'i rgyan//rnam 'phrul klu thabs ca' u ta ra//'dod yon lha mo'i tshogs kyis bskor//kun tu spyan dmar rdo rje'i

112b4(rgyud): ru ka~M ba mu kha/a kar Sha yar k+Sha na gaM gu ru hU~M phaT/snying po'i sngags so//oM pad ma ra ga ma ku r+ya ra ka~M ru lu ru lu hU~M phaT/nye ba'i snying po'o//dkyil 'khor chen po'i phyogs bzhi ru//lcags kyu lcags sgrog zhags pa dril bu rnams//

180a3(rgyud): men mos byed u ka ra//yid g.yo las can sems par gyur pa ni//ye shes dbang gis thugs rjes 'gro la g.yo//don mdzad gong 'brang ye shes dbyings su mkhyen//de phyir tshul khrims chen po kyi kya

180a5(rgyud): 'dzin yid la byed par 'gyur//ye shes dbyings rtogs drangs pa nyin mo banyal//'gro mdongs rmongs la thugs rjes mtshan mo ldan//ye shes thugs rjes drangs pa'i u lu ka/brtags pa'i dngos 
180a6(rgyud): la rtse gcig ting 'dzin 'gyur//mnyam nyid mtha' bral dbyings las mi g.yo ba//rtog gi spyod yul byis pas mi mthong ba//de phyir sbeb byed 'phra men ba ya ta//las 'bras bden la yid ches dang gyur

180a8(rgyud): lhag mthong ye shes dbyings nyid mnyam 'gyur 'dzin//zhi gnas gces par 'dzin pa mi 'phrogs pa//de phyir 'phra men 'dzin ma tsa ta ma//'dris pa'i dngos po ma brjed dran par gyur//

291a3(rgyud): sku gsung thugs nyid dang //yon tan phrin las sna tshogs ston//phyi ma'i dus na rig pa 'dzin//skyes bu brtson ldan rnams kyi ni//gnyis su 'dzin par yod mi bya//yul snang phyi nang ma lus kun//rang rig sems kyi rgyal 
291a4(rgyud): por sgrub//de ni ma bcos yul mi bral//ma chad ma lhung bdag po med//gnyis med mnyam nyid lhun gyis grub//des 'gyur spang blang rtsol sems bral//ma lus rtsa ba snying gi srog/de nyid shes bya rab tu blang //dbang gi rgyal 
291a5(rgyud): po 'char bar 'gyur//gal te de dang dregs pa can//so so rang rgyud ldan pa yi//gnyis 'dzin yid kyis rtog spyod cing //mtshan ma rab tu sgom byed par//rkang med khams gsum sgrod 'dod cing //mig med stong gsum 
291a6(rgyud): lta dang mtshungs//bdud bzhi dga' bskyed par byed//de bas rigs mchog ma rnams kyis//gsang bdag nga nyid shes par gyis//zhes bka' stsal nas//nyid kyi sku gsung thugs kyi dkyil 'khor chen po chos nyid 
291a7(rgyud): de bzhin nyid spos dang bral ba'i dbyings su thim pa mdzad do//'jig rten gyi sde dpon chen po 'khor dang bcas pa chos sku mthar thug tu sgrub par gdams pa'i le'u ste bcu gcig pa'o//dregs pa can gyi sde dpon
